- **sb:analyze**: Mine conversations into structured lessons (Context/Insight/Why/When/Pitfalls/Examples/Related), takeaways, action items, and tags. Uses `claude -p` with Sonnet by default. Usage — `/sb:analyze` (all unanalyzed), `/sb:analyze &lt;sid_prefix&gt;`, `/sb:analyze --force` (re-analyze even completed ones). ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/analyze.md) · ★1)
- **sb:ask**: Ask a natural-language question about your vault. Retrieves the most relevant notes (lessons, topics, conversations) lexically, then synthesizes an answer with cited [[wikilink]] sources via `claude -p`. Usage — `/sb:ask "what did I learn about X?"`. Supports `tag:#area/sub` filters in the query. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/ask.md) · ★1)
- **sb:ask-highlights**: Hallucination-proof retrieval — returns only verbatim vault lines matching a query (quotes, highlights, bullet claims) with file:line provenance, never generated prose. The safe complement to /sb:search. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/ask-highlights.md) · ★1)
- **sb:backfill**: One-shot import of historical Claude Code conversations from `~/.claude/projects/*.jsonl` into the vault. NOT run automatically. Usage — `/sb:backfill [--days N | --all] [--dry-run]`. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/backfill.md) · ★1)
- **sb:bases**: (Re)generate Obsidian Bases database views over the vault — Lessons, Projects, Conversations, Tasks, Memory. Idempotent; writes .base files into 00_Dashboard. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/bases.md) · ★1)
- **sb:challenge**: Argue against a note using your own prior notes — a skeptical devil's-advocate pass that appends a ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/challenge.md) · ★1)
- **sb:clean**: Vault cleanup. Dry-run by default; pass `--apply` to hard-delete. Removes empty conversations, garbage tags, stale session-map entries. Reports project slug mismatches to migrate separately. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/clean.md) · ★1)
- **sb:connect**: Suggest links between the current (or a named) note and existing vault notes. Usage — `/sb:connect` (uses current session's conversation) or `/sb:connect --file &lt;path&gt;`. To accept, run `/sb:connect --accept &lt;comma-paths&gt; [--theme &lt;name&gt;]`. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/connect.md) · ★1)
- **sb:consolidate**: Bound vault growth — dedupe lessons, flag stale conversations + orphans, promote durable lessons into harness memory. Dry-run by default; --apply to merge/archive (never hard-deletes). ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/consolidate.md) · ★1)
- **sb:daily**: Show today's second-brain activity — conversations updated, lessons created, tasks added/done, topics touched. No LLM, instant. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/daily.md) · ★1)
- **sb:dashboard**: Regenerate the Life-Dashboard homepage (00_Dashboard/Home.md + Today.md) — embedded Bases (review queue, tasks, decisions, people, meetings), Dataview recent lessons/zettels, habit streaks, and memory/remember highlights. Points the homepage plugin at Home.md. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/dashboard.md) · ★1)
- **sb:decision**: Capture an architecture decision (ADR) as an AI-first vault note — numbered Context/Decision/Consequences/Usage under 02_Projects/&lt;slug&gt;/decisions/ plus a global 11_Decisions copy. Use for "why did we choose", ADRs, and council verdicts. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/decision.md) · ★1)
- **sb:distill**: Distill a note into atomic, source-anchored claims — each claim cites the numbered source block it came from; unsourced claims are dropped and reported. Writes a verified:false distillation note to 08_Insights/ with sources preserved verbatim. Use to compress a long lesson/topic into traceable facts. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/distill.md) · ★1)
- **sb:emerge**: Surface emergent patterns across lessons/zettels/topics — clusters notes by shared tag or repeated title token and drafts a verified:false synthesis page in 08_Insights/ for each cluster of 3+ notes lacking one. Dry-run by default; --apply writes. Use to compound scattered notes into higher-order insights. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/emerge.md) · ★1)

([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/eval.md) · ★1)

([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/export.md) · ★1)
- **sb:find**: Fuzzy filename/title lookup across the vault. Usage — `/sb:find &lt;fragment&gt;`. Fast, no LLM. For full-text content search use /sb:search; for Q&A use /sb:ask. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/find.md) · ★1)
- **sb:graduate**: Promote a captured idea into a real project — ensures 02_Projects/&lt;slug&gt;/ (INDEX + kanban), seeds kanban cards from the idea's bullets, and flips the idea to status: graduated with a backlink. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/graduate.md) · ★1)

([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/graph.md) · ★1)
- **sb:habit**: Habit tracker — log a habit as done today (with streak) or list all habits and their current streaks. Use for "did X today", "track habit Y", "show my streaks". ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/habit.md) · ★1)

- **sb:idea**: Capture an idea into 16_Ideas/ (type: idea, status: captured). List bullet points in --body and they become the graduation checklist. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/idea.md) · ★1)
- **sb:ingest**: Write a supplied set of notes into the vault (generic, skill-agnostic). Reads a JSON payload from stdin or --payload &lt;file&gt; and writes each note as a typed project note, optionally refreshing an open-issues section. Used by the sutra orchestrator to land parsed repo artifacts in the vault; usable directly for any structured note payload. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/ingest.md) · ★1)

([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sb/commands/init.md) · ★1)

- **sb:search**: Full-text search the second-brain vault via the Obsidian CLI. Usage — `/sb:search &lt;query&gt;`. Supports Obsidian search syntax (`tag:#x`, `path:foo`, `file:bar`). - **screen-spec**: Generate a screen specification from a Figma frame using MCP tools (get_design_context, get_screenshot). Produces a spec doc matching the SCREEN_SPEC.md template with layout sketch, components used, spacing observed, data dependencies, copy, accessibility notes, interactions, and error states. Use when documenting a specific screen. Do not use when the screen has no Figma frame or when the spec already exists. For 6 or more screens, use screen-spec-fleet. Once screens document raw values, use extract-foundations-from-screens to converge tokens. ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/screen-spec.md) · ★6)

- **screen-spec-fleet**: Orchestrator-workers variant of screen-spec. Dispatches N Sonnet workers (1 screen each) in parallel to generate per-screen spec docs from Figma frames, then merges SCREEN_MAP.md and routes.md from per-worker partials. Use when screen count &gt;= 6 AND Figma MCP is available AND foundations + SCREEN_MAP exist. Do not use for single screens (use screen-spec), when foundations are missing (use design-bootstrap first), or when frames have not been enumerated yet (caller must supply screen_number+slug+persona+node_id per screen).
